## Question Replied
**Timestamp**: 2026-11-17T16:32:19Z
**Event**: QUESTION_REPLIED
**Session**: 066b3674-47bf-44db-9ca5-1e1f0bbe4a45
**Question**: I propose four coarse-grained Units with a vertical-slice first Unit. U1 (`u1-backup-foundation`) will integrate the configured-instance admin path, durable job/persistence, worker, archive integrity, and a database-only backup slice; it must label that slice incomplete and never present it as a complete/restoreable backup until required Auth and Storage coverage is added. U2 (`u2-complete-backup`) adds verified Auth and Storage capture and complete package/download behavior. U3 (`u3-restore-preflight`) adds safe upload, archive validation, compatibility preflight, explicit replace confirmation, and recovery-point checks without target mutation during preflight. U4 (`u4-restore-execution`) adds coordinated restore, post-restore validation, rollback/recovery-required outcomes, and job/audit history. The DAG is U1 → U2 → U3 → U4; these edges mean build/test dependencies, not an economic delivery recommendation. No production deployment is implied; localhost only. Does this decomposition plan look right?
**Reply**: Approve Plan
**Options**: ["Approve Plan","Revise Plan"]

---

**Timestamp**: 2026-11-17T16:33:21Z
**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED
**Stage**: units-generation
**Decision**: Units Generation decomposition plan: four coarse-grained vertical-slice Units, U1 foundation, U2 complete backup, U3 restore preflight, U4 restore execution; linear build/test dependencies, localhost only.
**Options**: Approve Plan,Revise Plan
